# Architecture — kanevas

RPG management system (lore, campaigns, session reports, permissions, maps). What it enables,
for whom and through which screens: `docs/parcours.md`, `docs/ecrans.md`; its data: `docs/donnees.md`.

This document has two parts: **today**, the map of the code as it stands; **the target**,
the architecture of the whole product, set by the epic's scope and implemented slice by
slice (on `main`, the target describes only what has been built).

# Today

Since `kanevas-premiere-fiche`, the foundation (one Fastify application, Node 20, TypeScript,
`GET /healthz`, an OIDC mechanism, an image published by CI) carries the **first business
function**: accounts, universes, members, sheets and sections, with their permissions; sessions
and stub mode; the React frontend that displays them (home, universes, members, lore, sheet).
It also includes the game system and, with `kanevas-suivi`, session tracking: adventures (campaigns and scenarios, both sheets),
their preparation and reports; `kanevas-relier-chercher`, relations between sheets and search (within a type, then across all types since `kanevas-import-recherche`; FTS5 indexes, AD-63, AD-64, AD-118); attachments (storage on the volume, E-9 block);
`kanevas-assistant-membre`, each member's assistant: a conversation panel whose tools
call the same service functions as the routes, with the person's permissions (AD-73 to AD-78).
Maps and graphs (E-10, E-11) are built. `kanevas-monde` adds a proposed section update based on a session report, which the GM applies or abandons with one action (AD-79 to AD-82); `kanevas-images` adds generation of a portrait attached to a section (AD-88 to AD-90).
`kanevas-outils-catalogue` adds the catalogue of a tool, imported at the system then the universe level, by the screen or by a tool-scoped token (`services/catalogues.ts`, `services/import-catalogue.ts`, migration `0021-catalogues.sql`): detail and decisions K-1 to K-8 in « Detail of `kanevas-outils-catalogue` ».
`kanevas-recours-admin` adds instance administration (E-5, AD-86, AD-87).
`kanevas-outils-cadre` adds the tools framework (`src/outils/`, `services/outils.ts`, `routes/outils.ts`, migration `0017-scenarios-pivot.sql` *(`kanevas-scenarios-suivi`, AD-127; campaigns and scenarios become `aventure` sheets, tasks attached to the sheet, tables dropped)*, `0018-natures-relations.sql` *(`kanevas-fiches-liees-natures`, AD-136; `relations.type` → `qualificatif`, `nature` column; number taken at the merge of `main`, which already held 0017, AD-51)*, `0019-outils.sql`, routes `/api/univers/:id/outils…` and `/api/systemes/:id/{outils,responsable}…`, tab « Outils » of E-14/E-15, sidebar section « Outils »): detail and decisions C-1 to C-6 in « Detail of `kanevas-outils-cadre` »; no real tool yet, only the trial module `essai` in stub mode and in test.
`kanevas-outils-galaxie-lire` adds the first real tool, the read side of the galaxy map (module `swn`, tool `galaxie`, migration `0022-swn-galaxie.sql`, `src/outils/swn/`, `src/services/swn/`, screen E-21 in `frontend/src/outils/swn/galaxie/`, assistant tool `swn_galaxie_trajet`): detail and decisions G-1 to G-9 in « Detail of `kanevas-outils-galaxie-lire` »; like `essai`, the module registers only in stub mode and in test until a deployment lists it. Writing the map (place, route, zone, seed, import) is `kanevas-outils-galaxie-tenir`.

`kanevas-outils-vaisseaux` adds the ship designer (tool `swn/vaisseaux`, migration `0024-swn-vaisseaux.sql`, then `0028-swn-armes-classe-min.sql` for the munitions by hull class, `src/outils/swn/regles/vaisseau.ts`, `src/services/swn/{calcul-vaisseau,conceptions,catalogue-vaisseaux,section-vaisseaux}.ts`, screen E-22 in `frontend/src/outils/swn/vaisseaux/`, assistant tool `swn_vaisseau_calculer`): decisions V-1 to V-12 in « Detail of `kanevas-outils-vaisseaux` ». `kanevas-outils-vaisseaux-regles` adds the stale design and « Recalculer » (the core's staleness, read at each GET), the seven managed rule and example sheets generated by « Générer les fiches de règles… » (migration `0025-swn-fiches-generees.sql`, `src/outils/swn/regles/texte-regles.ts`, `src/services/swn/{fiches-regles,regles-hooks}.ts`, routes `…/outils/swn/vaisseaux/fiches-regles`), regenerated on proposal: decisions R-1 to R-9 in « Detail of `kanevas-outils-vaisseaux-regles` ». `kanevas-outils-format-evolue` amends the formats: ship masses are multiples of 0.5 (migration `0026-swn-masses.sql`, V-10) and a route carries an optional `type_lien` (migration `0027-swn-routes-type-lien.sql`, T-4, T-8, T-9).

## Map

- `src/outils/`, `services/outils.ts`, `routes/outils.ts`, `frontend/src/outils/`, `frontend/src/ecrans/outils/`: the tools framework (`kanevas-outils-cadre`, see « Detail of `kanevas-outils-cadre` »).
- `src/services/catalogues.ts` (the only reader of a module's `StockageCatalogue`), `src/services/import-catalogue.ts`, `src/outils/essai/catalogue.ts`, `frontend/src/ecrans/outils/catalogue/`: the catalogue of a tool and its import (`kanevas-outils-catalogue`, see « Detail of `kanevas-outils-catalogue` », K-1 to K-8; routes: table K-7).
- `src/services/sections-gerees.ts`, `src/db/migrations/0020-sections-gerees.sql`, `frontend/src/ecrans/fiche/bandeau-gere.tsx`: managed sections, the one caller of the `ecrireContenu` lock (`kanevas-outils-sections-gerees`, see « Detail of `kanevas-outils-sections-gerees` »).
- `src/server.ts`, `src/app.ts`: startup and assembly of the Fastify app
  (`buildApp`), the repository's only application; all future routes register
  there.
- `src/config/env.ts`: environment reading (zod); the only exception is `FRONTEND_DIR`, read by `src/routes/frontend.ts` (test setting).
- `src/routes/health.ts`: `registerHealthRoutes`, `GET /healthz`.
- `src/routes/auth.ts`, `src/services/oidc.ts`: OIDC login and callback; the callback opens the
  session (`src/services/session.ts`, AD-56) and creates the account on first sign-in.
- `src/routes/frontend.ts`: serves the frontend build (`FRONTEND_DIR`, `dist/public`) behind the session guard; `src/routes/erreurs.ts`: translates service errors into HTTP responses; `src/routes/pages.ts`: stub HTML pages.
- `src/routes/bouchon.ts`: stub mode (AD-55), absent from the route table without `KANEVAS_STUB`.
- `src/services/assistant/`: a role's tool catalogue (AD-74), the `AgentTransport` port and its
  `bouchon` and `claude-agent` adapters (AD-73, AD-78), availability (AD-77) and
  request orchestration (AD-75), the token (`jeton.ts`) and its connection session (`connexion-claude.ts`, AD-112; `scripts/faux-setup-token.mjs` in stub mode); `src/routes/assistant.ts`: the two routes. No SQL queries
  in this module (AD-2, AD-27).
- `src/db/`: opening the SQLite file, `migrations/0001-*.sql`, `0002-systemes.sql`, `0003-suivi.sql`, `0004-pieces-jointes.sql`, `0005-relier-chercher.sql`, `0006-illustrations.sql`, `0007-cartes.sql`, `0008-propositions.sql`, `0009-plafond-contenu.sql` (100,000 ceiling, AD-106), `0010-fil-contrat.sql` (tokens, batches, source keys, AD-103), `0011-contenu-public.sql` (`sections.contenu_public`, section index rebuilt, AD-114), `0012-liens.sql` (`liens` table, target indexes and `relations (cible_fiche_id)`, AD-116), `0013-types.sql` *(`kanevas-import-types`, AD-120; number 0013 assigned when the epic resumed, reassigned if another slice goes first (AD-51); rebuilding `fiches`)*, `0014-alias-etiquettes.sql` *(`kanevas-import-recherche`, AD-118; aliases, labels, title index rebuilt)*, `0015-mediatheque.sql` *(`kanevas-import-mediatheque`, AD-117; `fichiers`, `fichiers_sections`, `fiches.illustration_fichier_id`, migration of attachments and illustrations)*, `0016-import-suivi.sql` *(`kanevas-import-reprise`, AD-121; `cle_origine`, `dernier_import`, `version_importee` on campaigns and scenarios)*, `0017-scenarios-pivot.sql` *(`kanevas-scenarios-suivi`, AD-127)*, `0018-natures-relations.sql` *(`kanevas-fiches-liees`)*, `0019-outils.sql` *(`kanevas-outils-cadre`, AD-121 (module declaration); `outils_systeme`, `outils_univers`, `systemes_jeu.responsable_id`; AD-51)*, `0020-sections-gerees.sql` *(`kanevas-outils-sections-gerees`)*, `0021-catalogues.sql` *(`kanevas-outils-catalogue`, AD-123, AD-130; `jetons_outils`, `lots_import.outil`, the two `essai_catalogue_*` tables)*, `0022-swn-galaxie.sql` *(`kanevas-outils-galaxie-lire`; the four `swn_galaxies`, `swn_systemes`, `swn_routes`, `swn_zones` tables; number taken at the merge, AD-51)*, `0023-swn-import.sql` *(`kanevas-outils-galaxie-tenir`, AD-104; `version_importee` on `swn_systemes`, `swn_routes` and `swn_zones`; number taken at the merge, AD-51)*, `0024-swn-vaisseaux.sql` *(`kanevas-outils-vaisseaux`; `classe_min` of `swn_catalogue_armes`)*,  `0029-swn-zones-plages.sql` *(`kanevas-outils-format-zones`, B-84; `swn_zones.plages`)*, runner (AD-14).
- `src/e2e/`: end-to-end tests, real server in stub mode and driven browser (Playwright, skipped if missing); E-5: `administration*.test.ts`.

  functions that read or write data (AD-2); `src/routes/`: thin `/api` routes; `univers.ts` and `instance.ts` carry universe and instance routes; `import.ts` (token-based import API, `/api/import/v1`) and `acces-api.ts` (E-18, session-based: `GET|POST /api/univers/:id/jetons`, `POST …/jetons/:jid/revoquer`, `GET …/lots`).
- `frontend/`: React/Vite application (AD-57); `frontend/src/ui/tokens.css` and

  (`Cadre.tsx`, `Barre.tsx`) is shared by all screens; `/demo-composants` (stub mode only, 404 otherwise) shows the components (AD-92 for fonts and icons).
- `src/services/systemes.ts`: catalogue, attach, create and attach, templates; editing
  a universe is in `src/services/univers.ts`; their routes are `src/routes/systemes.ts`
  (AD-83 to AD-85, AD-94). Screens: E-14 (« Paramètres »), E-16 (« Systèmes de jeu », `ecrans/systemes.tsx`), E-15 (« Système de jeu », `/systemes/:sid`, `ecrans/systeme.tsx`; the old `/univers/:id/systeme` address redirects through `ecrans/systeme-ancien.tsx`) and the « Système de jeu » block of E-3.
- Instance administration: `src/services/instance.ts`, its routes `src/routes/instance.ts`
  (`/api/instance`, AD-86, AD-87) and screen E-5 (`frontend/src/ecrans/administration.tsx`).

- `src/services/{preparation,comptes_rendus}.ts` and `src/routes/suivi.ts`: session tracking
  (AD-33, AD-34, AD-46, AD-47, AD-60, AD-61, AD-122 to AD-127). Campaigns and scenarios are sheets
  of type `aventure` (migration 0017, AD-127; the status is in the sheet's payload): no remaining
  `campagnes`/`scenarios` table or service. Preparation tasks (`taches_preparation.fiche_id`) and
  reports (`compte_rendu` sheets, `charge.campagne_id`) belong to an adventure sheet; the GM
  guard uses the sheet's universe, never a supplied universe id. Screens: the sheet's
  « Préparation » and « Comptes-rendus » blocks (`frontend/src/ecrans/fiche/registre-blocs-fiche.ts`,
  sheet block registry by type, role and rank, distinct from section blocks, AD-124), E-13, three
  E-3 blocks (ongoing campaigns, latest reports, preparation for the GM) and the « Campagne » line of
  E-9 (`fiche/lignes/` registry). E-6 and E-7 no longer exist.

- Media library (`kanevas-import-mediatheque`, AD-111, AD-117): `src/services/stockage.ts` (write, read, delete a file on the volume),
  `src/services/fichiers.ts` (`ajouterFichier`, `fichierLisible`, list, panel, impact, joins, illustration; sole writer of `fichiers*`)
  and `src/routes/fichiers.ts`. It replaces `pieces-jointes.ts` and `illustrations.ts` (AD-65 to AD-67, AD-93 amended). Screens: E-19 `frontend/src/ecrans/mediatheque.tsx`,
  « Pièces jointes » block, editor's « Fichier » and illustration of E-9, E-8 grid.
- `src/bouchon/depart.ts` and `src/bouchon/demo/`: demonstration world seeded in stub mode into a database without universes (through service functions); `KANEVAS_SANS_SEMIS=1` disables it.
- `kanevas-images` adds `src/services/images/` (the `GenerateurImage` port and its adapters,
  AD-88, AD-90), the GM catalogue's `generer_image` tool (AD-89) and the « Image attachée » block of E-12;
  the Docker image bundles `codex`.
- `src/services/llm/`: LLM transports (`transport.ts`, `anthropic-transport.ts`,
  `claude-agent-transport.ts`), reused from Antre-du-maitre, wired nowhere.
- `kanevas-cartes-graphes` adds `src/services/cartes.ts` (create, read under the reader's permissions, configure,
  place, remove; map background through `stockage.ts`), its `/api` routes (`src/routes/cartes.ts`) and screens E-10 and E-11
  (`frontend/src/ecrans/cartes.tsx` and `carte.tsx`; their components and graph layout are in the
  `ecrans/cartes/` and `ecrans/carte/` directories); AD-68 to AD-72.
- `Dockerfile` (multi-stage, `node` user) and
  `.github/workflows/docker-publish.yml` (checks the `tests-complets` attestation, then GHCR image: CI runs no tests).

Volume `/data`: `kanevas.db` (SQLite, WAL), `session.key` (session secret, 0600), `claude/oauth-token` (assistant token set through administration, 0600, AD-112; with an in-memory database, hence no volume, it goes under the temporary directory and does not survive a restart) and
`attachments/` (media library files, one per UUID, AD-65; `attachments/tmp/` for uploads
in progress, emptied at startup except with an in-memory database, where the directory is shared).

## Routes `/api`

The tools framework's routes (`/api/univers/:id/outils…`, `/api/systemes/:id/outils…`, `/api/systemes/:id/responsable…`) are tabulated in « Detail of `kanevas-outils-cadre` », C-5.

All guarded by the session (401 without a session), except `/api/auth/*` and `/api/import/v1/*`, which opens only with a Bearer token (AD-103) and ignores the cookie. Service errors are
`introuvable` 404, `refuse` 403, `invalide` 400, `conflit` 409 (`section_modifiee`, `nom_pris`, `gabarit_modifie`, `scenario_modifie`, `proposition_appliquee`, `relation_existante`, `limite_relations`, `limite_pieces`: 100 attached files per section (AD-111) — 409 for a GM, 403 `refuse` for another member (AD-117), `fiche_deja_placee` and `carte_pleine` for maps; 413 `fond_trop_lourd` for a background over 25 MB; 400 `fichier_vide` for an empty attachment (an empty map background is a 400 `fond_invalide`); `auto_relation` is a 400 `invalide`). Removing the last
GM returns `invalide` 400 with the reason; so does section content over 100,000 characters (AD-91). A service error body is `{message}` (plus `code` when the service supplies one); an `/api/...` address with no matching route (for example a route removed by AD-94) returns 404 with the « Page introuvable. » HTML page, rather than JSON. The server listens on `0.0.0.0` (`-p 3001:3001` suffices).
In stub mode, `POST /connexion-bouchon` expects a form-urlencoded body `compte=<identifiant>`.
The rows citing AD-93 or AD-94 belong to `kanevas-illustrations`, built by that slice;
they replace the old `GET /api/univers/:id/systeme` and `…/systeme/gabarits*` routes.

| Route | Role |
|---|---|
| `POST /api/apercu` | `{texte}` → `{html}`: Markdown rendering of a text, without reading or writing anything; 400 above 100,000 characters (AD-113). Optional context, flat in the body or under `contexte`: `{nature: 'section', univers_id, mode}` (`mode`: `joueur` or absent); with `nature: 'section'`, integer `univers_id` required (400 otherwise), unknown universe or non-member account: 404, and the GM block is rendered only for a GM outside `mode: 'joueur'` (AD-114) |
| `GET /api/moi`, `POST /api/auth/logout` | account identity, groups and limits (`limites.contenuSection`, AD-91); end session |
| `GET /api/auth/config`, `GET /api/auth/oidc/login`, `.../callback` | OIDC (public) |
| `GET\|POST /api/univers`, `GET /api/univers/:id` | account's universes (with its role, `systeme: {id, nom}` or `null` (AD-94) and, in the list, `nbMembres` for the E-1 cards); creation |
| `PATCH /api/univers/:id` | name and description (GM) |
| `DELETE /api/univers/:id` | *(AD-119, `kanevas-import-tenir`)* delete the universe (GM, outside Player mode): body `{nom}` matched exactly, otherwise 400 `nom_different`; 204 |
| `GET /api/univers/:id/types` | *(`kanevas-import-types`, AD-120)* product list read for this universe, in list order, including `compte_rendu`: `[{type, slug, singulier, pluriel, nouveau, aucun, icone, reglable, active, origine, forcage}]` (`origine`: `univers`, `systeme` or `defaut` — who decided; `forcage`: `active`, `desactive` or `null`); the GM also gets `systeme` (`true` or `false`: the system's decision; `null`: no system, or the system has no row for this type — do not use it to tell whether the universe has a system) and `nbFiches` (all sheets of the type, which the GM can all read); any member |
| `PUT /api/univers/:id/types/:type` | *(AD-120)* `{etat}`: `active`, `desactive` or `suivre`; returns the row as above (GM; 400 `type_inconnu` or `type_non_reglable` for `compte_rendu`) |
| `GET /api/univers/:id/sous-types?type=` | *(AD-110, AD-120)* `[{sousType, nb}]`, case-insensitive alphabetical order: subtypes of sheets **of the type that the caller reads** (the GM, outside Player mode, reads them all); serves the E-8 filter and E-9 completion (`?mode=joueur` reads as a Player); any member |
| `GET /api/systemes` | systems attached to a universe of which the account is a member, by name: `[{id, nom, nbUnivers, entrees: {regle, creature, objet}, mesUnivers: [{id, nom, role}], peutEcrire}]` — never a universe of which the account is not a member (AD-84, AD-94) |
| `GET /api/systemes/catalogue`, `POST /api/systemes` | catalogue (id, name pairs), read by a GM of at least one universe (E-14); system creation |
| `GET /api/systemes/:sid` (`:sid` integer; `?type` = `regle` (API default; the screen requests `creature`), `creature` or `objet`, otherwise 400; `?curseur`) | the system, `nbUnivers`, `mesUnivers`, `peutEcrire`, and `gabarits` (entries of the type, 100 at a time) with `suivant` (next-page cursor or null); same 404 as an unknown id if not attached to any of the account's universes (AD-94) |
| `POST /api/systemes/:sid/gabarits`, `PUT /api/systemes/:sid/gabarits/:gabaritId` | add, edit with the version read (AD-85): GM of at least one attached universe; 403 for a reader who is not a GM; 404 for someone who cannot see it |
| `PUT /api/systemes/:sid/types/:type` | *(AD-120)* `{active}`: enables or disables the type for the system; same permission as templates (GM of at least one attached universe; 403 for a reader who is not a GM; 404 for someone who cannot see it); `GET /api/systemes/:sid` additionally carries `types: [{type, active}]` |
| `PUT /api/univers/:id/systeme`, `POST /api/univers/:id/systeme-nouveau` | attach (`{systemeId}` or `null` to detach; 204 without a body), create and attach (201) (GM) |
| `GET\|POST /api/univers/:id/membres`, `PATCH\|DELETE .../membres/:compteId` | members (GM) |
| `GET /api/instance/univers`, `GET\|POST /api/instance/univers/:id/membres`, `PATCH\|DELETE .../membres/:compteId` | instance admin (`parents` group, AD-86, AD-87): `{id, nom, nbMembres}`; universe members, add (201), role, remove (204); outside the group, 404 like an unknown address |
| `GET /api/instance/assistant/jeton`, `POST /api/instance/assistant/jeton/connexion`, `POST …/jeton/code`, `DELETE …/jeton/connexion` | *(kanevas-import, AD-112)* instance admin only (404 otherwise): assistant token state (`pose`, `poseLe`, `source`, `assistant` = AD-77 availability, never the value), start connection (`connexionId`, address, `expireLe`), code (`{connexionId, code}`; 200, 400 `connexion_echouee`, 410 `connexion_expiree`), cancel (204) |
| `GET\|POST /api/univers/:id/fiches` (`?type`, `?q`, `?curseur`, *`?statut` with `type=aventure`*, `?lie=<fid>`, `?nature`, `?descendants=1` *(AD-130, `kanevas-fiches-liees-recherche`)*, and `?etat=la\|disparu` *(AD-132)*; *the POST also takes `parent` and `sections`, AD-128*) | paginated list (100), `q` (empty or over 100 characters: 400; without letters or digits: empty list) searches within the type, or across all types without `?type` (AD-63, AD-101); `?etiquette=` filters by label, `?mode=joueur` reads as a Player (AD-118); *(kanevas-fiche-mj-seul, B-57)* `?visibilite=joueurs\|mj` filters by the derived player visibility, GM out of player mode only (otherwise 400), and (the creation response `POST` excepted: it carries no such field, read it back with a `GET`) each sheet carries `visibleJoueurs` for that GM (absent for a player and in player mode); creation (GM); *(kanevas-deplacer-arborescence, AD-129)* `?horsDe=<fid>` (with `q`, GM outside player mode only, otherwise 400) leaves out that sheet and all its descendants |
| `PUT .../fiches/:fid/etiquetage`, `GET /api/univers/:id/etiquettes` | a sheet's aliases and labels replaced together `{alias, etiquettes}` (GM outside Player mode); suggested labels `[{nom}]` (readable sheets only), `?mode=joueur` on both (AD-118) |
| `GET .../fiches/:fid` | sheet (with `visibleJoueurs` for the GM outside player mode, B-57) and readable sections; `?mode=joueur` reads as a Player; 404 if no section is readable, except for the GM outside Player mode, who also opens a sheet with no section (`visibleJoueurs` false) (the screen translates it to « Aucune section n'est visible des joueurs. ») |
| `GET .../fiches?type=T&parent=racine\|<fid>` | *(AD-115, `kanevas-import-arbre`)* tree of a type: `type` is required with `parent`; `racine` returns sheets of the type the account reads whose parent is null, unreadable or of another type, `<fid>` the **same-type** children of that sheet the account reads (404 if `<fid>` is unreadable); same page of 100 and same cursor as the list; each sheet additionally carries `aDesEnfants` (a readable same-type child exists) and « `dans: {id, titre}` » or `null` (its parent, when readable and of another type); without `parent`, these two fields are not returned; *(B-57)* `visibleJoueurs` and `?visibilite=` apply to it as to the list |
| `GET .../fiches?lie=<fid>&nature=<key>&descendants=1` | *(kanevas-fiches-liees, B-65, AD-130)* the list with the linked-sheet conditions, all optional and combined by AND with `type`, `sousType`, `etiquette`, `q`, `parent` : `lie` the target sheet, `nature` a key of `GET …/natures` (absent = any relation, free ones included), `descendants=1` includes same-type descendants down to 50 levels through `descendantsDe` (a bounded recursive `UNION` CTE) ; symmetric natures match either direction ; both sheets and the carrying section must be readable ; each row may carry `via: {id, titre}`, the readable other end of the relation, ordered by title then id, absent when a qualifying direct link exists ; `lie` on a sheet the caller does not read, or of another universe, answers `200` with an empty page ; `nature` or `descendants` without `lie`, an unknown nature, a `lie` that is not an integer, or a `descendants` other than `1` : `400 Requête invalide.` ; same page of 100, same cursor, same order |
| `GET .../fiches/:fid/enfants` (`?curseur`) | *(AD-115)* « Contient »: children the account reads, all types, by type (product list order) then title; 50 per page, `{enfants: [{id, titre, type, sousType}], suivant}` (each child also carries `visibleJoueurs` for the GM outside player mode, B-57); 404 if the sheet is unreadable; never a count of what is hidden; *(kanevas-deplacer-arborescence, AD-129)* each child also carries `aDesEnfants` (it has at least one child, of any type, that the caller reads) |
| `GET .../fiches/racines` (`?curseur`) | *(kanevas-deplacer-arborescence, AD-129)* the universe root for « Déplacer sous… »: the sheets **without a parent**, every type, in the order, page size, cursor and shape of `enfants` (`{enfants: [{id, titre, type, sousType, visibleJoueurs, aDesEnfants}], suivant}`) ; GM outside player mode only (a player, or the GM in player mode: 403) |
| `PATCH .../fiches/:fid`, `POST .../fiches/:fid/type` | *(AD-120)* `PATCH` `{titre}` (1 to 120 characters, AD-119; GM outside Player mode), `{sousType}` (text of 1 to 60 characters, or `null` to remove it) and/or `{parent}` (sheet id or `null`, AD-115; 200 with the sheet); `{statut}` (`en_preparation`, `prete`, `en_cours` or `terminee`, adventures only, GM outside player mode, AD-124; another type or value: 400); an unknown key, or none of these keys: 400); `POST …/type` `{type, pj?, sousType?}` changes the type (GM; 400 `meme_type`, `type_inconnu`, `type_non_reglable` — a session report cannot change type, and no sheet can become one); *(`kanevas-fiches-liees`, AD-132)* `{disparu}` (boolean; personnage, créature, faction, famille, lieu, objet, vaisseau; GM outside Player mode; 400 on another type) |
| `GET .../fiches/:fid/mentions?apres=<n>` | *(kanevas-import-liens, AD-100, AD-116)* the next 50 readable sheets citing this sheet through links or free relations (natured relations belong to `liees`): `{mentions: [{ficheId, titre, type, pourquoi: [{genre: 'lien', section} \| {genre: 'relation', libelle}]}], suite, restantes}`; the first page travels with `GET .../fiches/:fid` (`mentions`, `mentionsSuite`, `mentionsRestantes`: the N in « Afficher les N suivants »); `?mode=joueur` reads as a Player; same 404 as the sheet |
| `POST .../fiches/:fid/sections`, `PUT .../fiches/:fid/ordre` | add, order (GM) |
| `DELETE .../fiches/:fid`, `GET .../fiches/:fid/suppression` | *(AD-119)* delete the sheet (GM outside Player mode; 204); impact summary counted by the E-9 dialog before deletion |
| `GET\|PATCH\|DELETE .../sections/:sid` | read; title and audience (GM); remove (GM) |
| `GET\|POST .../sections/:sid/relations`, `DELETE /api/univers/:id/fiches/relations/:rid` (without `:fid`) | readable relations of the section `{relations: [{id, nature, qualificatif, cible: {id, titre, type, charge?}}]}` (AD-64, AD-136; `charge: {pj}` for a personnage target, `kanevas-relation-badge-pj`; empty `nature` = free relation, `cible.type` is the sheet type); link `{cibleFicheId, nature?, qualificatif?}` (201; `qualificatif` of 0 to 80 characters, required without a nature; `type` accepted as a synonym of `qualificatif` for one version); 400 `nature_incompatible`; remove (204), GM only, 404 for a player |
| `GET /api/univers/:id/fiches/:fid/liees` (`?nature`, `?descendants=1`, `?curseur`, `?mode=joueur`) | *(kanevas-fiches-liees, AD-130)* the sheets linked to the sheet by a nature, read backwards, grouped : `{profondeurAtteinte, groupes: [{nature, libelle, types: [{type, total, totalAvecDescendants}], fiches: [{…ligne de E-8, qualificatif, via?: {id, titre}}], suivant}]}` ; counts and lines under the two guards of AD-64 ; `services/liees.ts` returns five lines per group, or 50 after the numeric offset `curseur` with `nature` ; unreadable sheet: 404 ; `descendants` on a place only (accepted without effect on other types) ; `profondeurAtteinte` at level 50 |
| `GET /api/univers/:id/natures` | *(kanevas-fiches-liees)* the product's list of natures with their labels both ways and their pairs of types |
| `POST .../fichiers` | *(AD-117)* add to the media library (multipart, one file; fields `section`, `maniere` (`joint`\|`texte`), `blocMj`, `fiche` **before** the `fichier` field); visibility is never read from the client, it is deduced from the location; 201 |
| `GET .../fichiers` | caller's readable list; `nature`, `visibilite`, `usage`, `q`, `tri`, `apres` |
| `GET .../fichiers/:fid/detail`, `GET .../fichiers/:fid/impact?action=reserver` or `…=supprimer` (400 otherwise) | panel (readable usages, `apresUsage`); what the GM loses by reserving or deleting, under actual permissions |
| `GET .../fichiers/:fid` (`?v=`) | bytes, sole bytes route, actual permissions (no mode); same 404 as an unknown address |
| `PATCH\|DELETE .../fichiers/:fid` | rename or change visibility `{nom, visibilite}` (GM); delete (204, GM) |
| `POST .../sections/:sid/fichiers`, `DELETE .../sections/:sid/fichiers/:fichierId` | attach `{fichierId}` (201; at most 100, `limite_pieces`), remove reference (204) |
| `PUT\|DELETE .../fiches/:fid/illustration` | set (GM): either `{fichierId}` (a media library image; 400 `pas_une_image`), or multipart (one `fichier` part, added to the media library, `fichier_vide`); remove (204). Bytes are read through `GET .../fichiers/:fid?v=<jeton>`; the list and sheet return `illustration: null` or `{id, jeton}` (`type` and `taille` appear only in the `PUT` response) (AD-93 amended, AD-117) |
| `PUT /api/univers/:id/fiches/:fid/sections/:sid/contenu` | write `{contenu, version}`; 400 if content > 100,000 characters (checked after permissions, before version); 409 if `version` is stale |
| `GET\|POST /api/univers/:id/fiches/:fid/taches`, `PUT /api/taches/:tid` | *(kanevas-scenarios-suivi ; replaces `/api/campagnes/:cid/taches`)* preparation `{categorie, libelle}` of an adventure; tick `{faite}`. GM of the adventure's universe only, 404 for anyone else (AD-47) |
| `GET /api/univers/:id/propositions/:pid` | caller's proposal: `{id, universId, sectionId, ficheId, crId, source (`compte_rendu` or `recalcul`), outilLibelle, contenuActuel, contenuPropose, versionOrigine, etat, creeLe, appliqueeLe}`; `etat`: `en_attente`, `perimee` or `appliquee` (AD-81). Requesting GM only, 404 for anyone else (AD-79) |
| `POST /api/univers/:id/propositions/:pid/appliquer`, `.../abandonner` | apply returns the same view as `GET` (200); 409 `section_modifiee` or `proposition_appliquee`, nothing written. Abandon returns 204 (409 if applied). No creation route: the proposal originates from the assistant tool (AD-48) |
| `GET /api/univers/:id/assistant`, `POST .../assistant/messages` | availability and message submission (details below, AD-73 to AD-78) |
| `POST /api/univers/:id/comptes-rendus` | `{campagneId, titre, texte?}`; any member (AD-61); returns the sheet — *`kanevas-scenarios`*: `campagneId` is the id of an adventure sheet (campaign or scenario) the caller reads |
| `GET .../comptes-rendus` (`?campagne`, `?curseur`), ~~`GET .../campagnes/:cid/comptes-rendus`~~ (*removed by `kanevas-scenarios`*; `?campagne=<fiche id>` serves the block) | readable session reports, newest first, at most 100 per page and `suivant`; `?mode=joueur` |
| `GET\|POST /api/univers/:id/cartes` | list of readable maps (`?curseur`); create `{titre, forme}` (`forme` required in JSON; the « illustrée » default belongs to the form and multipart path; `illustree` \| `graphe`), or multipart with `titre` and `forme` **before** `fichier` (background of an illustrated map). GM outside Player mode only (AD-72) |
| `GET\|PATCH /api/univers/:id/cartes/:cid` | `lireCarte`: map, elements and links already filtered (AD-68); `?mode=joueur` reads as a Player (403 `mode_joueur` for the GM if the map is not visible); configure `{titre?, visible?}` |
| `PUT\|GET /api/univers/:id/cartes/:cid/fond` | replace background (multipart, `fichier` field, image of at most 25 MB, AD-69); read it (account's actual permission, no mode) |
| `POST /api/univers/:id/cartes/:cid/elements`, `PATCH\|DELETE …/elements/:eid` | place `{ficheId, x, y}` (`x` and `y` required on an illustrated map, from 0 to 100 — out-of-bounds values clamped to the edge —, forbidden on a graph: `{ficheId}` only); move `{x, y}`; remove (204, the sheet stays) |

Request bodies (JSON): `POST /api/univers` `{nom, description?}`; `POST .../membres`
`{username, role}` (`role`: `mj` \| `joueur`; `username` is the exact identifier); `PATCH
.../membres/:compteId` `{role}`; `POST .../fiches` `{type, titre, charge?}` (`charge`: object, optional unless the type requires it — `personnage` needs `{"pj": bool}`, `compte_rendu` a `campagne_id`; shapes in `docs/donnees.md`);
`POST .../sections` `{titre, contenu?}` (`contenu`: text, same ceiling; the screen sends only `titre`); `PUT .../ordre` `{ids}` (all the sheet's section ids);
`PATCH .../sections/:sid` `{titre?, joueursLisent?, joueursEcrivent?, auteurLit?, auteurEcrit?,
auteurId?}` — `auteurId` is the **numeric account id** (members' `compteId`), `null` for
no author; `PUT .../contenu` `{contenu, version}`. API names use camelCase, database names
snake_case (`docs/donnees.md`).

## Invariants

- **The version has a single source**: the Docker build-arg `APP_VERSION`, derived
  by CI from the pushed semver tag (outside a tag, the value is not a semver: only an image built
  from a tag carries one). `/healthz` and the published image tag therefore
  cannot diverge. `package.json` deliberately has no
  `version`.
- **`/healthz` is public** (alongside `/api/auth/*` and login pages, AD-15), as plain text `kanevas <version>`, without data.
  `GET /api/auth/config` (`{oidcEnabled}`) is public too; no other public
  route exposes content.
- **The OIDC callback authenticates an identity, nothing more**: it opens a session carrying
  the identifier and groups, and creates the account; **no universe role comes from Authelia**
  (AD-9), it is read from the members table on every request. Without the four
  `OIDC_*` variables (or with only some of them), login and callback return 404; an empty
  or invalid value causes startup to fail.
- **Twenty-four tables and two search indexes, no ORM**: `comptes`, `univers`, `membres`, `fiches`, `sections` (migration
  0001), `systemes_jeu`, `gabarits` (migration 0002), `taches_preparation` (migration 0003, attached to a sheet by 0017; `campagnes` and `scenarios` are dropped there), `pieces_jointes` (migration 0004, emptied by 0015 and no longer read), `relations`, `recherche_fiches` and `recherche_sections` (FTS5, migration 0005); three illustration columns on `fiches` (migration 0006), `cartes` and `elements_carte` (migration 0007), `propositions` (migration 0008, `kanevas-monde`); `jetons_api` and `lots_import`, plus source keys on `fiches` and `sections` (migration 0010, `kanevas-import-fil`); `sections.contenu_public` and the rebuilt `recherche_sections` index (migration 0011, `kanevas-import-secrets`, AD-114); `liens` (migration 0012, `kanevas-import-liens`, AD-116); `types_systeme` and `types_univers`, and `fiches` rebuilt without a type `CHECK` (migration 0013, `kanevas-import-types`, AD-120); `fiche_alias`, `etiquettes` and `fiche_etiquettes`, and `recherche_fiches` rebuilt as `(titre, alias, sous_type)` (migration 0014, `kanevas-import-recherche`, AD-118); `fichiers`, `fichiers_sections` and `fiches.illustration_fichier_id` (migration 0015, `kanevas-import-mediatheque`, AD-117; `fichiers_attente`, created by the same migration, written by `assurerFichier` and sheet imports, `kanevas-import-fichiers`; the AD-93 illustration columns remain, unused); migration 0009 (`kanevas-import-markdown`) rebuilds `gabarits`, `scenarios` and `propositions` to raise their CHECK to 100,000 characters; numbers 0001 to 0007 are final, 0008 to 0018 are provisional until the epic merges into `main` and subsequent numbers are assigned at merge time (AD-51; `docs/donnees.md`). No SQL queries outside `src/services/` and `src/db/` (the stub seed, `src/bouchon/`, reads through `db.prepare` to find the identifiers it has just created).
- **Every route except `/healthz`, `/api/auth/*`, `/api/import/v1/*` (guarded by a Bearer token that ignores the cookie, AD-103) and, in stub mode, `/connexion-bouchon` is guarded by the session**; under `/api`, a
  missing session returns 401, elsewhere it redirects to login (AD-15).
- **Only one route calls a model: the assistant** (`src/routes/assistant.ts`, through `repondre`, AD-73), with the subscription
  token `CLAUDE_CODE_OAUTH_TOKEN`; the `services/llm/*` text transport is wired to nothing. No `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` secret is deployed.
  The two routes (under the session guard, AD-15): `GET /api/univers/:id/assistant` returns `{disponible, catalogue: 'mj'|'joueur'}`;
  `POST /api/univers/:id/assistant/messages`, body `{message, historique: [{role: 'user'|'assistant', content}]}`, returns
  `{reponse, evenements: [{type, libelle, cible}]}`; an error returns `{message, code}` (codes from AD-75 and AD-77).
- OIDC client: `client_id` `kanevas`, callback
  `https://kanevas.berard.me/api/auth/oidc/callback`, issuer
  `https://auth.berard.me`. These values are shared with the client
  declaration in `k8s-home-lab`: changing them here requires changing them there.

## Decisions and rejected options

- **New repository, selective reuse from Antre-du-maitre (AD-10)**: Fastify
  bootstrap, OIDC, LLM transports, Dockerfile pattern and CI, without Prisma or the
  business domain. Rejected: forking Antre-du-maitre.
- **OIDC wired from the foundation**, rather than deferred: AD-10 includes it among what
  is reused in the foundation. `claude-token.ts` (admin setup-token window) was not reused in the
  foundation; it is reused by `kanevas-import-jeton-assistant` (AD-109, AD-112).
- **No canary page**: the frontend starts with the first business page (AD-57).
- **Public repository, MIT licence**: the public GHCR package avoids a pull-secret and
  Renovate auto-bump; MIT in the absence of a shared licence policy across the fleet.
  Reversed only if the code must become confidential.
- **Version read from `package.json`**: rejected, two unsynchronised sources.
- In the cluster (`tantive`; Authelia is on `homenode`) the pod runs as `runAsUser: 0` (the `games` apps' convention for
  a writable hostPath): the image's `node` user (uid 1000) is
  tolerated, not required. Under uid 1000 it would need a hostPath writable by that user.


# The target

> Built to date: renaming and deleting a sheet, deleting a universe (`kanevas-import-tenir`, AD-119), the sheet import API and E-18 (`kanevas-import-fil`), files by key and their pending references (`kanevas-import-fichiers`), the GM block `:::mj` (`kanevas-import-secrets`, AD-114), sessions, stub mode, tables and their service functions,
> game systems and their templates, relation natures and qualifiers, search by link (advanced E-17), the « gone » mark and the Famille type (`kanevas-fiches-liees`, AD-130 to AD-134 and AD-136, and the « Fiches liées » block of E-9), relations and search across all types, by alias and label (E-17, `kanevas-import-recherche`), tracking (campaigns, scenarios, preparation, session reports), screens E-1 to E-11, E-13, E-14, E-15 and E-16 (E-9 with its « Pièces jointes » block), sheet illustrations, maps and graphs (E-10, E-11, « Cartes visibles » block of E-3), sheet types and the tree, `[[…]]` links, the universe's media library (E-19, files with their own permissions, migration 0015), file storage on the volume, and the member's assistant (E-12; its tools: search, read, write in a section, and for the GM generate an image that enters the media library and is attached to a section), and proposed section updates based on a session report (`kanevas-monde`: `proposer_mise_a_jour` tool, `propositions` routes, « Mise à jour proposée » block). The rest
> is the target of subsequent slices. The `kanevas-import` epic (scope proposed on 2026-10-11) adds
> AD-95 to AD-120, the import API and its security, deployment and stub entries.

## Components, and who talks to whom

```
browser (React, one SPA)  ──HTTP──▶  Fastify backend (one application)
                                         ├─ HTTP routes ─┐
                                         ├─ agent tools (GM | Player) ─┤─▶ service functions ─▶ SQLite + /data/attachments
                                         ├─ LLM provider (assistant)
                                         └─ codex exec (images), as a subprocess
Authelia (OIDC): identity only.

import script (outside the product, on Monsieur's Mac)
      ──HTTPS, Bearer──▶  /api/import/v1/univers/:id  (separate scope: token, never a cookie)
                                         └─▶ the same service functions (AD-2, AD-104)
```

- **Browser**: one React application (`react-router`, AD-16), served by the backend; it
  talks only to the backend, never to the database or disk. The assistant thread lives in the page
  (AD-28).
- **Fastify backend**: the only access point to data, disk, the LLM provider and
  Codex (AD-4). Every route is guarded by the session (AD-15): 401 under `/api`, redirection elsewhere; only `/healthz`,
  `/api/auth/*`, `/api/import/v1/*` (Bearer token, AD-103) and, in stub mode, `/connexion-bouchon` are public.
- **Service functions**: the sole implementation of each read and write, with its
  guards (`peutLireSection`, `peutEcrireSection`, `peutVoirFiche`, `fichierLisible` from `services/fichiers.ts`).
  Routes and agent tools call them in the same way (AD-2): this makes human / agent
  symmetry verifiable.
- **Agents**: two tool catalogues, GM and Player, selected on the server according to the
  account's role in the current universe (AD-26, AD-74), behind the `AgentTransport` port (AD-73).
- **Images**: a `GenerateurImage` port, `bouchon`, `aucun`, `codex` adapters (AD-88 to AD-90; AD-45, AD-50, AD-55).

## Decisions

Numbers are stable. A withdrawn decision keeps its number, with what replaces it.

| AD | Decision |
|---|---|
| AD-1 | Nothing from Adestia's engine to store or serve data: it has no per-resource ACLs. |
| AD-2 | A single path to data: an agent tool calls only service functions that a route also calls. |
| AD-3 | Everything in TypeScript on Node. |
| AD-4 | Every HTTP route in the single Fastify application. |
| AD-5 | All structured data in the single SQLite file on the volume. |
| AD-6 | A sheet type is a versioned payload contract, validated in code, never a new table or new columns. |
| AD-7 | *Amended by AD-111 (`kanevas-import`, Monsieur's decisions of 2026-10-11): an attachment is a media library file, referenced by the section; secret denotes the file's visibility.* Attachments: bytes on the volume's disk, never in the database or through a static path; a route that rechecks permissions; no size limit. |
| AD-8 | Full-text search (FTS5) on titles and sections, maintained by the database (AD-21); no result without the permission filter. |
| AD-9 | Authelia says who; `membre` says what they can do. The instance admin (Authelia `parents` group) writes only the members table. |
| AD-10 | New repository; selective reuse from Antre-du-maitre: Fastify bootstrap, OIDC, LLM transports, Dockerfile and CI. |
| AD-11 | Game system: entity shared across universes; lore remains specific to each universe. |
| AD-12 | *Replaced by AD-18 and AD-19.* |
| AD-13 | Account created on first authentication (`preferred_username`). |
| AD-14 | Migrations: numbered SQL files, applied at startup, without an ORM. |
| AD-15 | Every route guarded by the session (signed cookie), otherwise 401 under `/api` and redirect to Authelia — in stub mode, to test account selection (AD-55). |
| AD-16 | A single frontend router, `react-router`; each slice registers its screens there. |
| AD-17 | Empty v1 payload for all types, except character (PC \| NPC) and session report (its campaign); content is in sections. |
| AD-18 | The GM reads and writes every section of their universe. |
| AD-19 | Section audience: player read and write, author read and write, four independent toggles. |
| AD-20 | A relation is carried by a section and inherits its visibility; its target must also be readable. |
| AD-21 | The search index is maintained by SQLite triggers, never by code. |
| AD-22 | A sheet with no readable section returns 404, like an unknown id. |
| AD-23 | `systeme_jeu` without a universe; a universe references it. |
| AD-24 | Templates: a generic typed table. |
| AD-25 | Reference data: read by members of an attached universe, written by its GMs. |
| AD-26 | The agent's tool catalogue is selected by the server according to role, never by the client. |
| AD-27 | Agent tools compose service functions in-process, without internal HTTP or their own SQL. |
| AD-28 | No conversation stored: the thread lives in the browser. |
| AD-29 | *Replaced by AD-122 (`kanevas-scenarios`, 2026-10-25).* Campaigns and scenarios: their own tables, outside sheets. |
| AD-30 | *Retired by AD-122 : the tree lists a campaign's scenarios.* The campaign list never carries scenarios. |
| AD-31 | *Replaced by AD-126.* Creating a campaign or scenario through the GM agent: the same functions as the routes. |
| AD-32 | *Withdrawn: replaced by AD-52.* |
| AD-33 | *Kept (`kanevas-scenarios`) : the route takes the id of an adventure sheet (AD-124).* A session report is created through a dedicated route, open to any member, which composes sheet and section creation. |
| AD-34 | *Kept (`kanevas-scenarios`) : « d'une campagne » reads « d'une aventure » (`?campagne=<fiche id>`) ; a CR names its adventure only to a reader who reads it.* A campaign's report list rechecks each report section by section. |
| AD-35 | Filename on disk: UUID; the original name is only metadata. |
| AD-36 | *Amended by AD-111 (`kanevas-import`, Monsieur's decisions of 2026-10-11): an attachment is a media library file, referenced by the section; secret denotes the file's visibility.* Attachment guard: its section's guard, and GM only if secret. |
| AD-37 | *Amended by AD-111 (`kanevas-import`, Monsieur's decisions of 2026-10-11): an attachment is a media library file, referenced by the section; secret denotes the file's visibility.* Streamed attachments; `nosniff`; images inline, everything else as downloads. |
| AD-38 | A map element is returned only if its sheet is readable (`peutVoirFiche`). |
| AD-39 | A GM's Player mode can only restrict: it shows what a player who authored no section sees. |
| AD-40 | A map background is an image uploaded to the map, stored on disk like an attachment and served by a route that rechecks map readability. |
| AD-41 | *`kanevas-fiches-liees` (B-69) : the link's label is the nature's, the qualifier after it ; a free relation keeps its qualifier.* Graph links are derived from relations on read. |
| AD-42 | Graph layout is computed in the browser. |
| AD-43 | Token and node: a single elements table, with shape imposed by the map type. |
| AD-44 | *Amended by AD-111 (`kanevas-import`): the generated image enters the media library (visible to players, the default) and is attached to the section by the first path's add function (AD-111), no longer by the attachment upload function.* The image tool checks write permission before generation, and attaches through the attachment upload function; one image per request. |
| AD-45 | Image generation behind a port; the adapter is selected by configuration (`codex`, or `bouchon` in stub mode, AD-55); without an adapter, the tool disappears from the catalogue. |
| AD-46 | *Amended by AD-124 : the table hangs on the adventure's sheet (`fiche_id`).* Preparation tasks: dedicated table attached to the campaign, closed set of categories. |
| AD-47 | *Kept, read « de l'aventure ».* Tasks' GM guard evaluated on the campaign's universe. |
| AD-48 | The agent proposes an update, never writes it; applying is a human action in the interface. |
| AD-49 | A proposal belongs to its requester, is applied once, and only if the section has not changed. |
| AD-50 | `codex` adapter: `codex exec` as a subprocess, `danger-full-access` (the pod is the sandbox), credentials in `CODEX_HOME` on the volume. |
| AD-51 | A migration gets its number when its slice merges; migration order is merge order. |
| AD-52 | *Replaced by AD-102 (`kanevas-import`): the sheets table is rebuilt once, without a type constraint.* The sheets table knows its seven types from creation; no slice rebuilds it. |
| AD-53 | **Every action a human initiates has a screen.** The agent is a second path, never the only one; what the agent proposes, a human applies from a screen. |
| AD-54 | The assistant accesses the model through Monsieur's Claude subscription, via the `claude-agent` transport (Monsieur's decision, confirmed on 2026-10-03 with knowledge of the following fact). Risk known and accepted by Monsieur: the Agent SDK documentation does not allow using the claude.ai login or its limits in a third-party product without Anthropic's agreement; Kanevas serves accounts other than his own. Credentials are set by Monsieur. |
| AD-55 | **Stub mode** (Monsieur's decision, 2026-10-03): `KANEVAS_STUB=1` starts Kanevas without any secret. Login uses test account selection instead of Authelia, the assistant answers through a scripted `bouchon` transport that calls the same tools, the image comes from a `bouchon` adapter that returns a fixed image. Everything else is real: database, permissions, routes, tools. A banner says so on every page; the application refuses to start in stub mode if even one `OIDC_*` variable is set. Tests and chain pods run in stub mode. |
| AD-56 | **Session**: a signed cookie (`HttpOnly`, `SameSite=Lax`, `Secure` outside stub mode and tests) carries the account id and groups, valid for 7 days; the signing secret is read from `SESSION_SECRET` if set, otherwise created once in `session.key` beside the database (`/data/session.key` in production, 0600) — sessions survive a restart without deploying a new secret. No revocable session in V1: removing a member takes effect on the next request because roles are not in the session (AD-9). |
| AD-57 | **Frontend**: React and Vite in `frontend/`, a single build served by the Fastify application (`@fastify/static`, fallback to `index.html` for any screen address, behind the session guard); the Docker image builds both. No server rendering, except pages that cannot follow the session: test account selection (AD-55), « Connexion refusée », « Connexion indisponible ». |
| AD-58 | *Replaced by AD-95 (`kanevas-import`): content is Markdown, rendered on the server.* **Section content as plain text**: paragraphs separated by blank lines, displayed as such; neither Markdown nor HTML. Rejected: Markdown (rendering to sanitise, editor choice) — can be reopened without a migration, the content is already text. |
| AD-59 | **Concurrent section writes**: each section carries an integer `version`, incremented on every content write; the write sends the version it read; if it is no longer current, it is refused (HTTP 409, code `section_modifiee`) and nothing is written. Same mechanism as B-21's section-changed check for proposals (AD-49). |
| AD-83 | *The always-through-a-universe access rule is replaced by AD-94 (`/api/systemes/:sid` routes, catalogue at `GET /api/systemes/catalogue`); the rest stands.* **Game system and attachment**: `systemes_jeu` (without a universe) and `gabarits`; the universe carries an optional `systeme_id` (AD-23, AD-24). In this original decision, access to a system **always went through a universe of which the account was a member** (`/api/univers/:id/systeme`); the catalogue provided names only. « Créer et rattacher » is a single transaction. Rejected: an `/api/systemes/:id` address guarded by attachment to one of the account's universes (a query per read, no gain). |
| AD-84 | **A system reveals nothing about neighbouring universes**: it returns the number of universes using it, never their names or members (AD-22). Clarifies AD-11: lore remains specific to each universe. Rejected: naming partner universes (scope mockup), which would disclose the existence of universes of which the account is not a member. |
| AD-85 | **Templates**: Markdown content (AD-95); concurrent writes refused through an integer `version`, like AD-59 (HTTP 409, code `gabarit_modifie`); type fixed at creation; no deletion (scope). Service functions take the actor and check the role in the universe **used for access**; they are the only writes, for routes and agent alike (AD-2). Rejected: structured fields (level, DEF, PV) — outside the defined scope (structured statistics). |
| AD-86 | **Instance admin**: the session's `parents` group (AD-56); the route derives an `ActeurInstance` (`compteId`, `admin`) from it, never an id read from the body. Instance service functions (`src/services/instance.ts`: list universes, list members, add, change role, remove) are **the only ones** accepting this flag, and they touch only `membres` (AD-9) and, for the instance universe list, each universe's id, name and member count (AD-87), without description: sheet, section and attachment services do not know it, so an admin without a role is an account without a role there (404, AD-22). B-3 and B-5 guards are the GM guards (same code, without the role guard). **Accepted risk**: the group is read at login and remains valid until session expiry (7 days); an account removed from the group retains administration until then. Rejected: rereading the group on every request (Authelia cannot be queried outside login). |
| AD-87 | **Instance routes**: `/api/instance/univers` (list: id, name, member count, nothing else) and `/api/instance/univers/:id/membres` (read, add, change role, remove). No instance route returns a content field; without the group, **404** with the same body as an unknown address (never 403: even the existence of administration is not revealed). Universe routes (`/api/univers/:id/…`) do not change: the admin is an account like any other there. Rejected: opening universe routes to the admin (a back door to content, against the scope's security rule). |
| AD-88 | **`GenerateurImage` port** (`src/services/images/`): `generer(description, signal)` returns the bytes of **one** image and nothing else. Adapters: `bouchon` (AD-55: a fixed PNG image; a description containing « échec » raises the generation error), `codex` (AD-90), `aucun`. Selected by configuration, read only by the configuration module: `bouchon` if `KANEVAS_STUB=1`; otherwise `codex` if the `codex` executable is present and `$CODEX_HOME/auth.json` exists (`CODEX_HOME` defaults to `/data/codex`); otherwise `aucun`. The result must bear the signature of an image Kanevas serves inline (AD-66), otherwise it is a failure. One engine at a time in the process (lock), one instance (AD-5). Rejected: a paid image-generation API (the brief forbids spending). |
| AD-89 | **The `generer_image` tool**: in the **GM** catalogue only (AD-74; a GM in Player mode keeps it, like the other tools), absent if the adapter is `aucun` (AD-45). Parameters: section id (as returned by `lire_fiche` and `chercher`) and description (at most 500 characters); account, universe and role are never parameters (AD-74). The executor, in this order: (1) refuses if the account cannot write the section — **before** generation, to avoid spending engine resources (AD-44); (2) refuses a description over 500 characters (« La description est limitée à 500 caractères. ») and, if the request already generated an image, a second one (« Une seule image par demande. »); (3) generates; (4) attaches through `ajouterFichier` (AD-111, AD-117 §6), joined to the section, named `image-<date>-<heure>.png`: visibility is deduced from the section (GM-only if players cannot read it) and the function rechecks permission, the 100 attached files limit and the section's state at write time; (5) returns the `image_attachee` event `{libelle, cible: {type: 'fiche', ficheId, sectionId, fichierId, description}}` (AD-76). Any failure returns an error result to the agent, **no event** and no file, never an exception that would lose the answer. If the request is abandoned (the client leaves), the engine is stopped and nothing is attached. **Assistant stub (AD-78)**: the rule « fais un portrait pour « S » d'« F » » (`chercher`, `lire_fiche`, then `generer_image` with the message as description) is registered **before** the « échec » rule: this is how a portrait request containing « échec » fails in the engine and says so, instead of raising the transport error. |
| AD-90 | **`codex` adapter** (AD-50): `codex exec` as a subprocess, `--sandbox danger-full-access` (the pod is the sandbox), in a disposable directory `/data/tmp/images/<uuid>`, with `CODEX_HOME` on the volume; the instruction requests an image and only its generation. Codex stores its images in `$CODEX_HOME/generated_images/<session>/` and does not always print the path: the adapter **lists the directory before and after** (AD-88 lock: only one engine at a time) and takes the new file; no new file is a failure. It deletes the file it read and the disposable directory. Deadline: **150 seconds** for the engine; AD-75 (120 s for the request) **does not count time spent in `generer_image`**, so a request with an image takes at most 120 + 150 seconds and the client waits 300 seconds. The Docker image bundles the `codex` executable, pinned version. Credentials (`auth.json`) are placed on the volume by Monsieur; the pod neither logs nor returns them. **Risk decided, checked on the first real call** (with Monsieur's credentials, as in AD-73): that `codex exec` actually generates an image under the subscription and stores it where we look; planned fallback: fix file detection; if the engine cannot generate under the subscription, real acceptance testing goes to arbitration, the stub stays intact. Rejected: reading the path from Codex output (not guaranteed); a disposable `CODEX_HOME` directory per request (credentials would have to be copied each time). |
| AD-91 | *Value raised to 100,000 characters by AD-106 (`kanevas-import`); the mechanism stands.* **Server-side section content ceiling**: the `sections` service refuses content over 20,000 characters (`String.length`), on addition and writing alike: HTTP 400 `invalide`, « Contenu trop long : 20 000 caractères au plus. », nothing written, version unchanged. The value lives in a single server constant (`MAX_CONTENU_SECTION`), returned to the frontend through `GET /api/moi` (`limites.contenuSection`): the screen does not copy it. Rejected: an `/api/limites` route (an extra round trip, `/api/moi` is already loaded by the screen shell); a configurable limit (outside this slice, the value can evolve). |
| AD-92 | **Bundled fonts and icons** (visual redesign): Fraunces, Newsreader and Inter come from `@fontsource` packages and icons from `lucide-react`, pinned versions, bundled by Vite and served by the application. No request to a third-party host: the instance remains self-contained and nothing leaks to a CDN on each page. A missing font falls back to its system stack. Rejected: Google Fonts and an icon CDN (network dependency, leaked visits); a custom-drawn icon set (inconsistent, requires maintenance). |
| AD-93 | *Amended by AD-111 (`kanevas-import`, Monsieur's decision of 2026-10-11): the illustration is a media library image file, referenced by the sheet; it is visible if the sheet **and** image are readable (the never-secret rule is dropped); its bytes are served through the files route, with the same token and cache.* **A sheet's illustration** (`kanevas-illustrations`, Monsieur's decision of 2026-10-06: each sheet must have an optional illustration image property). *Binds*: the E-8 list, E-9 header, sheets service, storage module (AD-65). *Prevents*: an illustration leaking more or less than the sheet, a grid downloading each image again on every visit, two ways to recognise an image. *Rule*: a sheet has **at most one** illustration, an optional sheet property (three columns on `fiches`, `docs/donnees.md`), **visible to whoever sees the sheet** (`peutVoirFiche`, actual permissions, no mode parameter) and **never secret**; **only the GM** sets, replaces or removes it. The type is **determined by the server from the signature** (PNG, JPEG, GIF, WebP, as in AD-66); any other content is refused, nothing written. Bytes go through AD-65's storage module (tmp then rename, same directory); permission is checked **before** reading the stream and **again** in the writing transaction; the replaced file is deleted after commit. **No server-side image processing**: the original is served, the browser crops it (`object-fit: cover`) in a fixed-size frame, and the grid loads its images as they approach (`loading="lazy"`). Routes (under `/api/univers/:id/fiches/:fid`): `PUT …/illustration` (multipart, a single `fichier` part; set or replace; 200 `{illustration: {jeton, type, taille}}`); `DELETE …/illustration` (204, even if there was none); `GET …/illustration` (optional `?v=<jeton>`; bytes, column's `Content-Type`, `Content-Disposition: inline`, `X-Content-Type-Options: nosniff`, `Content-Security-Policy: default-src 'none'; sandbox`). Errors: unknown or invisible sheet, or no illustration when reading → **404 with the same body** as an unknown id (AD-22, AD-67); someone who sees the sheet without being a GM → **403** on `PUT` and `DELETE`; empty file → 400 `fichier_vide`; unrecognised signature → 400 `pas_une_image`. `GET /api/univers/:id/fiches` (list and search) and `GET …/fiches/:fid` return `illustration: null` or `{jeton}`; the **token** is opaque (the disk name, changing on each replacement) and serves only to build the address `…/illustration?v=<jeton>`. **Cache**: if `v` is the current token, `Cache-Control: private, max-age=31536000, immutable` (the address changes with the image, nothing to invalidate); without `v` or with a stale token, `private, no-store`. Driving fact: without a size limit (AD-7) and with AD-66's `no-store`, a grid of 100 sheets would download up to 100 originals again on every visit; the token and lazy loading reduce the cost to once per image per browser. *Rejected*: a thumbnail **derived from attachments** (a section's first image) — rejected by Monsieur, and it would take the section's visibility rather than the sheet's; a **secret** illustration or one configurable by audience — rejected: the illustration is at title level, which is never secret, and an extra setting would be a second who-sees-what on the same sheet; a **linked table** — an extra entity for a cardinality a column carries; **server-side resizing** (`sharp`/libvips) — a native dependency in the image, computation in the pod and a second copy to maintain, for a gain that caching and `lazy` already provide on the local network (reopened if the grid is too heavy on a phone outside the local network); AD-66's `no-store` unchanged — the grid would download everything again on each visit; the token in the address without a permission check — the route always rechecks (AD-7); an illustration-specific **size ceiling** — rejected by Monsieur on 2026-10-06: no ceiling, as in AD-7; can be reopened if photos of several tens of MB arrive. |
| AD-94 | **The game system, a separate dimension** (`kanevas-illustrations`, Monsieur's decision of 2026-10-06: the game system becomes a separate dimension, rather than a sub-element of a universe). Replaces AD-83's always-through-a-universe access rule; clarifies AD-85 (the universe used for access). *Binds*: E-15, E-16, the E-3 block, E-14, systems service and routes. *Prevents*: a system screen inside a universe shell (breadcrumb « Lame d'Ébène › CoF Mini »); two definitions of who sees a system depending on the address; a list naming another account's universe. *Rule*: a system has **its own address**, guarded by **attachment to a universe of which the account is a member** (read) and **GM of at least one of those universes** (write: add, edit an entry) — the role is evaluated across all the account's universes attached to the system, never on a universe supplied by the caller; outside this guard, **404 with the same body** as an unknown id (AD-22); someone who reads without write permission receives 403 on write. What a response says about a system: its name, the **number** of universes using it, entry counts per type, and **only the account's universes** using it, with its role (AD-84 unchanged). Routes: `GET /api/systemes` → account's visible systems `[{id, nom, nbUnivers, entrees: {regle, creature, objet}, mesUnivers: [{id, nom, role}], peutEcrire}]`, by name; `GET /api/systemes/catalogue` → old catalogue (id, name pairs; a GM of at least one universe), read by E-14; `POST /api/systemes` (create) unchanged; `GET /api/systemes/:sid` (`?type`, `?curseur`) → the system, `nbUnivers`, `mesUnivers`, `peutEcrire` and its entries (100 at a time); `POST /api/systemes/:sid/gabarits` and `PUT /api/systemes/:sid/gabarits/:gabaritId` (version read, AD-85). `:sid` is validated by `idDeChemin` in the handler (strict integer, otherwise 404); the static `catalogue` route takes priority over `:sid`, so `catalogue` is never mistaken for an id. `GET /api/univers/:id` returns `systeme: {id, nom}` or `null` for the E-3 and E-14 block. Remaining under the universe, because they act on the universe: `PUT /api/univers/:id/systeme` (attach, detach) and `POST …/systeme-nouveau` (create and attach). Removed in the same slice: `GET /api/univers/:id/systeme` and `…/systeme/gabarits*` (frontend and backend ship in the same image, there is no client to preserve); the screen address `/univers/:id/systeme` redirects to `/systemes/:sid`. No new data: the guard is read from `membres` and `univers.systeme_id`. An agent tool reading a system would call these same guarded functions (AD-2); none does today. *Rejected*: keeping access through the universe (AD-83) — the breadcrumb and sidebar would make the system part of the universe, which Monsieur rejected; and a system page reached through two universes would have two addresses and two shells for the same content. AD-83's rejection (a query per read, no gain) no longer holds: **security is the same** (the attached-to-an-account-universe guard is exactly what access through a universe applied, and a guessed address responds like an unknown one), the extra query is a join on two small tables, and **the navigation model now requires it**. Also rejected: `GET /api/systemes` keeping its catalogue meaning with a parameter for my systems (one route, two response shapes and two permissions); naming a system's other universes (AD-84). |
| AD-60 | *`kanevas-scenarios` : « actives » reads « en cours » ; the rule holds for adventures (AD-123).* **Several campaigns in the same universe may be active**: changing a campaign's status touches no other. Rejected: only one active (setting a campaign to `active` would finish or suspend another without the GM knowing — a hidden side effect). Can be reopened without a migration. |
| AD-61 | **Creating a session report** is a single service function, open to any member: a transaction that writes the sheet and its « Compte-rendu » section (readable by players, written by its Player author, with no author if created by a GM). Sheet creation remains a GM permission; this function is the sole exception, for the `compte_rendu` type. |
| AD-62 | *Retired by AD-122 : a scenario's text is a section, with the sections' own version (AD-62 is no longer needed).* **Concurrent scenario writes**: same mechanism as AD-59 — an integer `version`, sent on write, refusal 409 with code `scenario_modifie` if it is no longer current. Two GMs can write the same scenario. |
| AD-63 | **Search is the list, plus a condition.** Searching within a type means listing that type (same function, same permission filter, same alphabetical order, same pagination) with a matching condition on the FTS5 indexes: the index delivers only candidates, never a result (AD-8). A sheet matches if all words are in its title, or all in the same section the account reads; prefix matching, case- and accent-insensitive; input is neutralised (each word quoted, quotes doubled, symbols without letters or digits discarded). Rejected: relevance ranking and snippets (a snippet is content to filter, a ranking betrays what is hidden); search across all types (outside scope, through the assistant) — *this rejection is lifted by AD-101 (`kanevas-import`), the rest stands*. Can be reopened without a migration. |
| AD-64 | *Amended by AD-136 (`kanevas-fiches-liees`) : a relation carries a nature and a qualifier ; the two guards hold.* **A relation is read under two guards**: the carrying section **and** the target sheet must be readable by the reading account (and mode); otherwise the relation is absent from the response, without a placeholder or count (AD-20, AD-22). Only the GM creates and removes it; its target is in the same universe, never its own sheet; removing a section removes it (cascade). Rejected: a relation-to-a-hidden-sheet marker (it reveals that a sheet exists); displaying incoming relations on the target sheet (not promised; the graph derives them, AD-41) — *this rejection is lifted by AD-100 (`kanevas-import`), both guards stand*. *Amended by `kanevas-relation-badge-pj` (2026-10-25)*: the target of a relation carries, besides `id`, `titre` and `type`, what its badge reads and nothing more — `charge: {pj}` for a personnage —, so that the Relations block shows PJ or PNJ exactly; the block never fails on a target without `charge` (the type's label then). Before, the screen read `charge.pj` on a target that had none and crashed on every relation towards a personnage (40 production sheets). |
| AD-73 | **The agent is an `AgentTransport` port, separate from the text transport** reused from Antre-du-maitre (designed for a turn without tools). Adapters: `claude-agent` (Agent SDK, Monsieur's subscription, AD-54) and `bouchon` (AD-78). For `claude-agent`: catalogue tools are exposed through an **in-process** MCP server named `kanevas`; empty `tools` (no SDK built-in tools: no files, shell or network); empty `settingSources` (the SDK loads no pod settings); `allowedTools` contains only the catalogue's `mcp__kanevas__<outil>`; `permissionMode` `dontAsk`; `maxTurns` 12; an `AbortController`; the `CLAUDE_CODE_OAUTH_TOKEN` token passed in the call's `env`, read only by the configuration module, never logged or returned. The port is narrow enough for a tool (images, proposals) to be added to the catalogue without changing it. Rejected: reusing the text transport (no tools); SDK built-in tools (a back door into the pod). Known, decided risk (not an open question): that the SDK accepts in-process tools with this token; this can only be established on the first real call, which takes place after acceptance testing (see the order below), and until then the `claude-agent` adapter is exercised only through a fake `query`. Order: the slice's acceptance testing runs in stub mode; the first real call runs on the deployed URL, therefore **after** the merge of `kanevas-am-deploy` (which declares an optional variable, harmless until Monsieur sets its value) and token setup. If this is false, the `claude-agent` adapter is reworked (reopening task `kanevas-am-transport`); the already-declared variable remains, without effect. |
| AD-74 | *`kanevas-scenarios-suivi` : `lister_campagnes`, `creer_campagne` and `creer_scenario` no longer exist in the catalogues (campaigns are listed through `chercher` with type « Aventure »).* *Amended by `kanevas-import`: `chercher` without a type calls AD-101's all-types search (no more loop over seven types); `lire_fiche` and `lire_section` return the reader's view (AD-96: without GM blocks for a non-GM, links resolved); a player's `modifier_section` and `ajouter_a_section` are refused on a section containing a GM block (AD-98); content written by a tool can reach 100,000 characters (AD-106). The rest stands.* **Two catalogues, selected by the server.** Player: `chercher` (one or more words, within a type or across all: without a type, the tool calls single-type search, AD-63, for each of the seven types and returns sheets by type then title, at most 20, stating when the list is truncated), `lire_fiche`, `lire_section`, `modifier_section`, `ajouter_a_section` (adds a paragraph after the content without copying existing text), `lister_campagnes`. GM: those, plus `creer_campagne`, `creer_scenario` (AD-31) and `proposer_mise_a_jour` (AD-80). A tool is a description, schema and executor calling a service function that the route also calls (AD-2); **account, universe and role are never parameters**: they come from the session and route address, and the actor is the account alone, without Player mode (a GM in Player mode keeps their GM catalogue). An account without a role in the universe has no catalogue (same refusal as an unknown universe). A refused tool returns the same result as the interface — « Introuvable. » for what is unreadable, « Vous ne pouvez pas modifier cette section. » for what is readable but not writable, « La section a changé depuis que vous l'avez lue. Relisez-la. » for a stale version (AD-59) — and adds no event (AD-76). Reads return the section's `version`. The system instruction says that the assistant has exactly the person's permissions, answers in French, and on « Introuvable. » says it cannot find the item, without asserting anything else. Rejected: a sheet creation or audience-setting tool (B-26 promises search, summarise, edit); a tool for maps and graphs (outside B-26: they are read on screen, not through the assistant). |
| AD-75 | **One request, one response**, without streaming or server state (AD-28 forbids storing the conversation): the client sends the message and the last 20 messages of the thread; limits: 2,000 characters per message, 20 history messages, 12 agent turns, 120 seconds (the `AbortController` stops the agent), **one request at a time per account** (in-process lock, single instance, AD-5, released on error and timeout). Errors: 400 (empty message, too long, history too long), 404 (account without a role or unknown universe), 429 `assistant_occupe`, 502 `assistant_erreur` (transport failure or timeout, no write announced), 503 `assistant_indisponible` (AD-77). Rejected: SSE streaming (resumption, cancellation, for a display benefit). Reopen if answers regularly exceed 30 seconds. |
| AD-76 | **What the agent wrote is stated.** Each successful write tool adds an **event** `{type, libelle, cible}` to the response: `section_modifiee`, `section_completee`, and `proposition_creee` (AD-80). The label is written by the server and names only what the person can read (scenario and campaign for a GM). The `cible` (type, ids) lets the client build the link. On screen, a **block registry** (`frontend/src/ecrans/assistant/blocs/registre.ts`): one row per event `type`; this slice registers the `ecriture` block, the `kanevas-monde` and `kanevas-images` slices register theirs without editing an existing block. |
| AD-77 | **Without a token, the assistant is unavailable and says so.** Availability: `bouchon` if `KANEVAS_STUB=1`, `claude-agent` if a token exists (file set through administration, otherwise non-empty `CLAUDE_CODE_OAUTH_TOKEN`; *amended by AD-109, AD-112*), otherwise none: the availability route returns `disponible: false`, sending returns 503 `assistant_indisponible`, neither the stub nor the SDK is called — never a silent fallback to the stub. The variable is optional in deployment: a missing secret breaks neither OIDC nor startup. The token is set by Monsieur (AD-54), in OpenBao or through administration (AD-109). |
| AD-78 | **The assistant stub is an ordered keyword script** (`services/assistant/bouchon.ts`): the first matching rule calls the caller's real catalogue tools and builds its answer from their results. Rules: propose a section update from a report (AD-80); read a section; append a paragraph; search and read sheets; generate a portrait (AD-89); simulate image failure for a portrait request containing « échec »; otherwise simulate a transport error for « échec ». Unmatched requests return `HORS_SCRIPT` (« Je ne sais répondre qu'à des demandes de test : chercher, lire, ajouter. »). A matched rule whose tool is absent refuses without writing. Campaign and scenario creation call the GM tool `creer_fiche` (AD-126); the scenario rule first searches the adventure parent by exact title. |
| AD-79 | **A proposal is one row, belonging only to its requester** (`docs/donnees.md`): proposed content, section, source session report and the read section's `version` (AD-59) — this is the scope's fingerprint, without a second mechanism. It can only be read, applied and abandoned by its requester, still a GM of the universe; for any other account it responds like an unknown address (AD-22). **One pending per (requester, section)**: a new one replaces the old one in a transaction, bounding proposals made unreachable by reloading the thread (AD-28). Abandoning deletes it; once applied it stays, marked, and cannot be applied again. Rejected: a content fingerprint (a version suffices, it already exists); keeping abandoned proposals (nothing reads them again); a my-proposals list (outside scope: batch proposals, resumption after reload). |
| AD-80 | **`proposer_mise_a_jour` is a tool in the GM catalogue only** (AD-74): parameters `section_id`, `cr_id`, `version` (as returned by `lire_section`) and `contenu`; it calls the proposal-creation service function, which checks that the section belongs to the universe and the report is a readable session-report sheet, refuses empty content or content over 20,000 characters (*100,000 since `kanevas-import`, AD-106: AD-91's single constant*), and refuses a stale `version` like `modifier_section` (« La section a changé depuis que vous l'avez lue. Relisez-la. »). **The tool never writes into a section** and the catalogue has no apply tool (AD-48). Success: a `proposition_creee` event (AD-76) whose target is the proposal id and whose label names the sheet and section. Stub (AD-78): the rule « mets à jour la section « S » de « F » d'après le compte-rendu « C » » (S: section, F: sheet, C: report title) calls `chercher` (the report), `lire_section` then `proposer_mise_a_jour` with the current content followed by a paragraph « Mise à jour d'après « C ». »; placed before the « lis-moi la section » rule. |
| AD-81 | **Applying is a route, a GM action**: `GET /api/univers/:id/propositions/:pid` (proposal, current section, `etat`: `appliquee` as soon as `appliquee_le` is set, otherwise `perimee` if the section's `version` no longer equals `version_origine`, otherwise `en_attente`), `POST …/appliquer`, `POST …/abandonner`. Applying calls **the existing section write function** (AD-2, AD-59) with `version_origine`, and marks `appliquee_le` in the same transaction: if the section changed, 409 `section_modifiee`, nothing written; if already applied, 409 `proposition_appliquee`; abandoning an applied proposal also returns that conflict. Guards are those of a GM writing the section; account other than the requester, removed GM, different universe: 404. Rejected: applying without comparing versions (would overwrite an edit); merging the two texts (no safe way without interpretation). |
| AD-82 | **The block rereads from the server.** The thread keeps `proposition_creee` and its target, not the state: the `proposition` block in the E-12 registry (AD-76) reads the proposal on display and after each action, so an old block says « appliquée », « périmée » or « n'existe plus » according to the database. Content rendered as plain text (AD-58). *Amended by `kanevas-import` (AD-95): « Actuel » and « Proposé » are rendered as Markdown by the rendering function, for the requesting GM, with a « Rendu / Source » toggle; the assistant thread (messages, answers) remains plain text — it is not stored content.* The assistant does not know the current screen: the session report is named in the request. |
| AD-65 | *Amended by AD-111 (`kanevas-import`, Monsieur's decisions of 2026-10-11): an attachment is a media library file, referenced by the section; secret denotes the file's visibility.* **File storage and upload**: a module `src/services/stockage.ts`, without any concept of sections, writes a stream to `/data/attachments/tmp/<uuid>`, then moves it to `/data/attachments/<uuid>` (same volume: a rename); it reads as a stream and deletes. It also serves map backgrounds (AD-40). **`deposerPieceJointe(compte, mode, sectionId, flux, nom, secrete)` is the only upload function**: the route and image tool (AD-44) call it. It checks write permission **before** reading the stream, then again in the transaction writing the row (permission may be withdrawn during a long upload); a failure or cancellation writes neither row nor file. No size limit (AD-7), empty file refused, 50 attachments per section. Rejected: base64 in JSON (memory, 33% overhead), size limit (decision AD-7). |
